  - Current English: "Sour, and it ran fast ({rate} g/s): water rushed past the coffee. A finer grind gives it more contact; more water would only thin it."
- **missing** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin: under-extracted, with too little in the cup. A finer grind extracts more without diluting."
- **missing** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeakSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin, but the shot already ran slowly, so grinding finer risks stalling it. More heat or a longer soak extracts more instead."
- **missing** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourStrong`

  - Current English: "Sour but heavy: under-extracted and concentrated. More water through the puck fixes both."
- **missing** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter, and it ran slowly ({rate} g/s): too much contact. Coarsen the grind."

- **missing** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterWeak`

- **missing** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.living`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "One adaptive profile instead of many. It fills until the bed pressurises, soaks until pressure relaxes, then extracts by flow under a pressure ceiling. After each shot, Dial in proposes changes to soak, ceiling, flow and yield, applied with one tap."
- **missing** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.turbo`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **missing** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **missing** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.astringent`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "A drying finish usually means uneven extraction under high pressure. A 6 bar ceiling, as in the Turbo protocol (Cameron et al., 2020), protects the puck."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.astringentLowPressure`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Drying even at low pressure points to prep or an over-long tail rather than pressure."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sour`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour at a normal pace: pass more water. A longer ratio raises extraction without the uneven flow that grinding finer can cause (Cameron et al., 2020)."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourLongRatio`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour, but the ratio is already {ratio}. More water would mostly thin the cup, so improve saturation or extraction rate instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourBody`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Traditional Body style: resolve sourness with temperature rather than a longer, thinner shot."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitter`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter: cutting the ratio by about 0.2 trims the bitter tail of the shot."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterShortRatio`
  - Previous English: null

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour, and it ran fast ({rate} g/s): water rushed past the coffee. A finer grind gives it more contact; more water would only thin it."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin: under-extracted, with too little in the cup. A finer grind extracts more without diluting."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeakSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin, but the shot already ran slowly, so grinding finer risks stalling it. More heat or a longer soak extracts more instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourStrong`

- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterSlow`

- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterStrong`

- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterWeak`

  - Current English: "Turbo Shot"

  - Current English: "Blooming Espresso"

  - Current English: "Flow-Prioritized"
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.living`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "One adaptive profile instead of many. It fills until the bed pressurises, soaks until pressure relaxes, then extracts by flow under a pressure ceiling. After each shot, Dial in proposes changes to soak, ceiling, flow and yield, applied with one tap."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.turbo`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Your palate and the refractometer disagree. Trust the cup, and taste again."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "{count} flagged"

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Pace"

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Fast · {rate} g/s"
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.pace.normal`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Normal · {rate} g/s"
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.pace.slow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Slow · {rate} g/s"
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourFast`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour, and it ran fast ({rate} g/s): water rushed past the coffee. A finer grind gives it more contact; more water would only thin it."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin: under-extracted, with too little in the cup. A finer grind extracts more without diluting."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeakSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin, but the shot already ran slowly, so grinding finer risks stalling it. More heat or a longer soak extracts more instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour but heavy: under-extracted and concentrated. More water through the puck fixes both."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter, and it ran slowly ({rate} g/s): too much contact. Coarsen the grind."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and heavy: over-extracted and concentrated. A coarser grind takes out less."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and thin: the tail of the shot is adding bitterness and water. Stop earlier."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but thin: more coffee at the same ratio adds strength without changing extraction much."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weakBasketFull`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Thin, but the basket is already at its rated dose. A shorter ratio concentrates the cup instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.strong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but heavy: a slightly longer ratio opens it up."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.puckDropFresh`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Resistance dropped suddenly. This coffee is only {days} days off roast; trapped CO₂ makes uneven flow more likely, so prep carefully and give it a longer soak."

- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.living`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "One adaptive profile instead of many. It fills until the bed pressurises, soaks until pressure relaxes, then extracts by flow under a pressure ceiling. After each shot, Dial in proposes changes to soak, ceiling, flow and yield, applied with one tap."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.turbo`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Slow · {rate} g/s"
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourFast`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour, and it ran fast ({rate} g/s): water rushed past the coffee. A finer grind gives it more contact; more water would only thin it."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin: under-extracted, with too little in the cup. A finer grind extracts more without diluting."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeakSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin, but the shot already ran slowly, so grinding finer risks stalling it. More heat or a longer soak extracts more instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour but heavy: under-extracted and concentrated. More water through the puck fixes both."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter, and it ran slowly ({rate} g/s): too much contact. Coarsen the grind."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and heavy: over-extracted and concentrated. A coarser grind takes out less."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and thin: the tail of the shot is adding bitterness and water. Stop earlier."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but thin: more coffee at the same ratio adds strength without changing extraction much."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weakBasketFull`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Thin, but the basket is already at its rated dose. A shorter ratio concentrates the cup instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.strong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but heavy: a slightly longer ratio opens it up."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.puckDropFresh`

- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.living`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "One adaptive profile instead of many. It fills until the bed pressurises, soaks until pressure relaxes, then extracts by flow under a pressure ceiling. After each shot, Dial in proposes changes to soak, ceiling, flow and yield, applied with one tap."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.turbo`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

  - Current English: "Three shots of the same coffee, tasted side by side, so sour and bitter mean something concrete."

- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourFast`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour, and it ran fast ({rate} g/s): water rushed past the coffee. A finer grind gives it more contact; more water would only thin it."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin: under-extracted, with too little in the cup. A finer grind extracts more without diluting."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourWeakSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour and thin, but the shot already ran slowly, so grinding finer risks stalling it. More heat or a longer soak extracts more instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.sourStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Sour but heavy: under-extracted and concentrated. More water through the puck fixes both."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterSlow`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter, and it ran slowly ({rate} g/s): too much contact. Coarsen the grind."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterStrong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and heavy: over-extracted and concentrated. A coarser grind takes out less."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.bitterWeak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Bitter and thin: the tail of the shot is adding bitterness and water. Stop earlier."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weak`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but thin: more coffee at the same ratio adds strength without changing extraction much."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.weakBasketFull`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Thin, but the basket is already at its rated dose. A shorter ratio concentrates the cup instead."
- **needs review** — `insights.dialIn.reason.strong`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Balanced but heavy: a slightly longer ratio opens it up."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Resistance dropped suddenly. This coffee is only {days} days off roast; trapped CO₂ makes uneven flow more likely, so prep carefully and give it a longer soak."

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Living Profile"

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Turbo Shot"

  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Blooming Espresso"
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.preset.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Flow-Prioritized"
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.living`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "One adaptive profile instead of many. It fills until the bed pressurises, soaks until pressure relaxes, then extracts by flow under a pressure ceiling. After each shot, Dial in proposes changes to soak, ceiling, flow and yield, applied with one tap."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.turbo`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Cameron et al. (2020): a lower dose, a coarser grind and a 6 bar ceiling gave more even, more repeatable extraction. About 15 g in, 40 g out in roughly 15 seconds, for clarity and sweetness."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.blooming`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Scott Rao: fill quickly at 4 ml/s until 4 bar, rest 30 s with the pump off and the group sealed so the puck saturates and degasses, then percolate gently at 2 ml/s."
- **needs review** — `library.coffee.presetWhy.flowLed`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Schmieder et al. (2023): flow rate had the largest effect on the compounds in the cup, ahead of grind and temperature, though all were minor next to total yield. Holds a flat 1.8 ml/s and lets pressure taper as the puck erodes."

  - Current English: "Calibrate your palate"
- **needs review** — `library.calibrate.intro`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Three shots of the same coffee, tasted side by side, so sour and bitter mean something concrete."
- **needs review** — `library.calibrate.how`
  - Previous English: null
  - Current English: "Keep the dose at {dose} g and the grind where it is. Only the yield changes. Pull all three and taste them together."

- **needs review** — `library.calibrate.short.taste`
